Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on statistical_mechanics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 820. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 669 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 820 + O(1/n^669). Summing over all modes and applying the statistical_mechanics convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
